SECTION 1 — HARDWARE PLAYBACK CLASSIFICATION
====================================================================================================

Classification rules derived from measured hardware capability:
  SERVER  Intel i5-6260U / Iris 540 VA-API decode: H264 all profiles, HEVC Main
          8-bit, MPEG2, VC1, VP8. NO HEVC Main10, NO VP9, NO AV1.
  CLIENT  Fire TV Stick 2 (MT8127): H264 + HEVC hardware decode, both capped at
          1920x1088. 895 MB RAM. Output 1080p.

  GREEN  = client hardware-decodes directly; server never touches it.
  AMBER  = client cannot direct-play, but the SERVER can hardware-decode it,
           so transcoding is possible (still costs a scarce QSV session).
  RED    = neither can hardware-decode. Server falls back to SOFTWARE decode on a
           2-core 15W chip. This is the failure class.
